# Lokoja Life — Project Worklog

---
Task ID: 1
Agent: Main Agent (Super Z)
Task: STAGE 1 — Project foundation + main menu + character creation + game state

Work Log:
- Inspected existing scaffold: Next.js 16 + App Router + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui + Zustand (matches requested stack; Next.js replaces Vite as bundler)
- Created game architecture: `src/game/` (types, data, systems, store) + `src/components/game/` (Avatar, SceneBackdrop, NeedsBar, BottomNav, screens)
- Data-driven foundation: `config.ts` (central economy/balance — starting stats, needs engine params, XP curve, reputation tiers, transport, rent, business, weather), `avatar.ts` (skin/hair/style options), `locations.ts` (all 10 Lokoja locations with map coords + activities), `jobs.ts` (10 jobs, Stage 3 consumes), `foods.ts` (10 items, Stage 4 consumes), `flavor.ts` (names + menu tips)
- Systems: needs engine (clamps + hungry/starving consequences), progression (XP curve, level-ups with overflow, reputation tiers, naira formatter), versioned save system (localStorage, migration hook, Supabase-ready `toCloudPayload`), audio engine (WebAudio synth — click/chime/levelup/notify, zero assets)
- Zustand store: player + world state, startNewGame/continueGame, rest (nap/sleep with time advancement + daily weather roll), addCash/addXp/addReputation engine actions, debounced autosave (1.2s), notifications
- UI: warm Nigerian theme tokens in globals.css (green/gold/terracotta/cream), original layered SVG Avatar (8 hairstyles × 6 skin tones × 5 hair colors × 4 clothing styles × 6 colors), original SVG menu scene (Mount Patti + Confluence + keke + canoe)
- Screens: MainMenu (New Game/Continue/settings/reset), CharacterCreation (live avatar preview, 🎲 Nigerian name randomizer, sticky CTA), Dashboard (HUD header with XP + naira, condition summary, needs bars, skills, day/time/weather/location cards, Nap/Sleep actions, 5-tab bottom nav; Map/Work/Bag = honest ComingSoon panels with real data teasers, Profile = identity, reputation ladder, level unlocks, sound toggle, save now, exit)
- Root page.tsx: SPA screen router + boot save detection + flush save on pagehide/visibilitychange
- Verified via agent-browser at 390px/360px/1280px: menu → creation (customization + randomizer) → dashboard → nap (time+needs changed) → sleep (Day 2) → tabs → save → reload → Continue restored exact state. Zero console/page errors. Lint clean.

Stage Summary:
- STAGE 1 COMPLETE. Playable loop: create character → live in Lokoja (Day/N time, weather rolls, needs management via rest) → autosaved.
- Key decisions: Next.js instead of Vite (env standard, same React/TS/Tailwind/Zustand stack); single-route SPA with in-app screens (env requires `/` only); manual SaveSystem instead of zustand/persist (clean migration to Supabase); no fake features (Map/Work/Bag show honest "arrives in Stage N" panels fed by real data files).
- Next stage entry point: `LOCATIONS` data is ready for the Stage 2 map + movement; transport costs pre-tuned in `ECONOMY.transport`.

---
Task ID: 2
Agent: Main Agent (Super Z)
Task: STAGE 2 — Map + Movement + Transport

Work Log:
- Extended data layer (no rewrites): ECONOMY.transport reshaped to distance-priced fares (base+perKm) with minutesPerKm/energyPerKm; new TRAVEL config section (kmPerMapUnit, slotsPerTrip, per-transport rain multipliers, eventChance, fareRounding, transitionMs); locations.ts gained shortName, transport availability (Mount Patti excludes taxi) and travelModifier (Patti ×1.5 uphill, Confluence ×1.15)
- Extracted shared world clock to systems/world.ts (nextSlot/rollNewDayWeather/weatherForSlot/advanceWorldClock) — store rest/advanceTime refactored onto it; travel uses the SAME clock, no second time system
- systems/travel.ts: pure calculateTravel (distance→cost/time/energy/arrival, weather × location modifiers) + rollTravelEvent (30% chance, weighted, gated by weather/transport); travelEvents.ts data with 4 events (rain delay, go-slow traffic, keke refuel stop, quick-help tip)
- Store: travel() action with guards (locked/insufficient cash/energy → toast feedback), travelling state, arrival applies fare+energy+clock through systems, arrival + summary + event toasts, autosave; save.ts got defensive defaults (no version bump needed — world.currentLocation existed since Stage 1)
- MapCanvas.tsx: original stylised Lokoja SVG — Niger from NW + Benue from E meeting at Confluence, Mount Patti west mass, one bridge on Kpata-Patti road, laterite roads radiating from Ganaja Junction, zone tints, cartouche + compass; markers positioned from the same mapX/mapY data
- MapScreen.tsx: marker states (current ✓ ring / available / locked 🔒+grayscale), bottom-sheet location panel (description, activities with stage locks, distance line), travel confirmation rows (cost/time/energy per transport, disabled with reasons), travelling overlay; Dashboard: 📍 header + Travelling… state, Map tab live, rest gated to home
- Tests: scripts/test-travel.ts 17/17 passed (determinism, spec example Home→Kpata keke ₦290/8min/-2⚡, taxi ₦980/4min/-1⚡, rain ≤40% slower, Patti ×1.5, 90 pairs valid, event eligibility); browser: creation→map→keke/walk/taxi trips with exact cash/energy/time math, arrival toasts, reload persistence (confluence/day17/cash700), locked panels, live traffic event fired, rain rolled on Day 17 (walk 15→21min, keke 8→9, taxi 4), 360px + 390px + 1280px clean, zero console/page errors, lint clean
- Fixes during testing: rest card now correctly gated to Home (was usable anywhere)

Stage Summary:
- STAGE 2 COMPLETE. Player can explore all 10 Lokoja locations via walk/keke/taxi with config-driven fares, one shared clock, weather + location modifiers, travel events, and persistent location.
- Ready for Stage 3: jobs/hustles will read JOBS data (already complete) and can hook travel events into the opportunity system.

---
Task ID: 3
Agent: Main Agent (Super Z)
Task: STAGE 3 — Core Economy, Work & Opportunity System

Work Log:
- Extended (no rewrites): types.ts (Transaction/Reward/SkillId/ActiveOpportunity/WorkResult + player.skillXp, cooldowns, activeOpportunities, transactions, stats), config.ts (new WORK + HUSTLE tuning sections, GAME_META → Stage 3), jobs.ts (+optional cooldownSlots; photographer 2), world.ts (+absoluteSlot monotonic clock helper — cooldowns/expiry share the ONE clock, no second time system)
- New data: npcs.ts (10 NPCs w/ stable ids + homeLocationId + speech style — future phone/relationship registry), hustles.ts (17 personal hustles across all 9 work locations, each tied to an NPC + location id, with difficulty/skill/requirements/cash-range/cooldown/weight)
- New systems: economy.ts (canAfford, recordTransaction, pushTransaction cap 50, sums, describeReward — the ledger the future Bank reads), skills.ts (skill XP→level with level-scaled caps + banking), work.ts (checkRequirements w/ human reasons, calculateJobPay = base × skill bonus × mood, resolveJob always-pays + great crits, resolveHustle risk roll skill-vs-difficulty clamped, seeded-RNG injectable for deterministic tests), opportunities.ts (makeOpportunityInstance, isEligible dupe/cooldown/requirements gates, generateHustleOffers weighted w/ dry-chance, pruneExpiredOpportunities, resolveOpportunity, jobAsOpportunityMeta — board jobs speak the same opportunity language)
- Store: addMoney/removeMoney (ledger + stats), giveReward (one entry point for cash+xp+skillXp+rep, composes engine actions), addSkillXp (skill level-up toasts), workShift (location/requirement/cooldown/energy guards → working overlay → clock advance → payout → cooldown), searchHustles/acceptOffer/declineOffer/completeOpportunity/dropOpportunity/pruneExpired (full lifecycle), travel now records fare + event tips in the ledger; offers cleared on arrival (gist goes cold)
- Save v2: migration case 1→2 + defensive defaults for all new fields; verified in browser that a stripped v1 save loads and autosaves back as v2 with stats intact
- UI: WorkScreen.tsx (accepted-offer cards w/ NPC message + 🎯 live odds + expiry countdown + Do it/Take me there/Drop; hustle board w/ search cooldown; jobs here w/ pay estimates + confirm sheet; jobs elsewhere w/ deep-links; earnings section w/ today flow + recent ledger); Dashboard wiring (WorkTab live, skill XP bars on Home, Life-stats card on Profile, work-tab nav badge when offers wait); MapScreen (focus deep-link via state initializer, stage≤3 activity chips now live + work chips deep-link to Work tab)
- Tests: scripts/test-stage3.ts 48/48 (ledger math, skill caps+banking, pay scaling, seeded outcome determinism, fail/success paths, odds clamps, generation eligibility/dry-chance/location-binding, expiry pruning, cooldown day-crossing, 6-day work-loop economics); test-travel.ts regression 17/17; browser: full loop at 360/390/1280px — create→map deep-link→keke→shift (exact ₦ math 5000−290+970=5680)→cooldown block→search→accept→do (₦510 in range)→fail path (₦0 + lesson XP)→away-offer Take-me-there→expiry pruned (2→0 after deadlines)→reload persistence (cash/offers/cooldowns)→v1 migration; zero console/page errors; lint clean

Stage Summary:
- STAGE 3 COMPLETE. The core loop works: travel to a place → work a shift or hear gist → accept → do it → money/XP/skill XP/reputation → ledger + stats → autosave. Jobs are reliable, hustles are personal and risky, skills grow visibly, cooldowns run on the one world clock.
- Architecture is phone-ready: opportunities are plain data (source: npc|board|phone|gist), NPCs are a registry, every offer references stable location ids the future 3D world will reuse, and the ledger awaits Banking. No UI coupling — the same opportunity shape can arrive via chat later without redesign.
- Key decisions: absolute-slot clock space for all cooldowns/expiry (no second time system); job cooldowns default from WORK config with per-job override; skill caps scale with player level and bank excess XP; hustle odds shown honestly on cards; offers are transient (not saved) while ACCEPTED opportunities persist on the player.
- Next stage entry point: FOODS data + eat activity keys are waiting for Stage 4 shops/inventory; mood now meaningfully affects pay, giving needs management economic weight.

- Checkpoint FIRST: git tag `inc3-baseline` @ 3206a6b.
- ACCURATE GEOGRAPHY (the core): all 10 mapX/mapY pins remapped to a north-up real-Lokoja layout — confluence NE of town (map 68,12), Patti west (16,34), Kpata centre-east near-river, university NE (Adankolo side), motor park south. RIVERS rebuilt to true courses: Niger in from NW curving east north of the city, Benue in from E, combined outflow flowing SOUTH along the town's EAST side (town between Patti and the Niger — matches the research). ROADS rebuilt as the named real spine: IBB Way, Murtala Mohammed Way spine, Ganaja road (hugging the west bank), Nataco/university road, confluence-viewpoint road, Kogi Bridge road (E crossing), southern bypass (Felele-side crossing), Patti foot road + switchback summit trail, GRA road, GRA–IBB link, Felele road. Confluence viewpoint re-nudged onto the SW bluff overlooking the actual meeting point. 4 contiguous traffic loops rebuilt from the new graph; vehicles now ride bridge decks (bridgeDeckY) across the bypass. 2 REAL auto-detected bridges (Kogi Bridge road + southern bypass); false crossings eliminated (Ganaja road pulled 13.2 m clear of the carve).
- 2D MAP REDESIGN (MapCanvas): full artistic redraw to the same accurate geography — north-up rivers with rotated labels, confluence swirl NE, Patti massif with contour ridgelines + summit rest-house mark + "458 m · summit trail" label, 11 named road paths, 2 drawn bridges, urban tint between Patti and the river, compass. Markers still positioned by the same mapX/mapY the 3D world uses — art and world can't drift.
- 3D WORLD DEPTH: GRASS — 3,200 deterministic instanced tufts (one draw call) with a moisture gradient (lush near rivers → dry savanna gold), slope-aware scatter + sunk bases so nothing floats on the heightfield's faceting. TREES — 280 with a riverine palm family (leaning trunks + radial fronds + crown nuts) alongside broadleaf savanna trees. Patti raised to ~30 u (≈2× the old mound), rocky slopes via slope-based vertex colouring. NEW LANDMARKS: Lord Lugard's Rest House on the summit (white bungalow, veranda, chimney), Township Stadium (oval stands, pitch, 4 floodlight pylons) south of the motor park, Central Mosque (dome + minaret) by the Kpata road, church hall near the university, 5 river boats (canoes + a laden ferry) drifting the real Niger/Benue/outflow courses with ping-pong loops.
- VIEWS: camera modes follow/orbit/drone (C key or HUD chip; drone = high overview rig + wider zoom + slower lerp), photo mode (V or HUD button — HUD chrome fades, minimal pill to exit), live north-up minimap (anchors as tinted dots, colour once discovered; player heading arrow; 8 Hz poll, zero 3D-frame work). playerPos now carries heading.
- DISCOVERY ("places found"): session-spanning localStorage set (WORLD3D.discoveryKey, NOT in the save); district arrival records + "new place discovered!" toast + chime; HUD chip "n/10 places found"; MapScreen gains the "Lokoja atlas" progress card (bar + still-out-there hints + completion line); map visits count too (travel IS discovering).
- STAGE 4 GOES LIVE — THE EATING LOOP: FOOD config (5 meals, ₦250–₦700, hunger/mood/energy deltas + groceries basket for Kpata); store.eatMeal — REAL removeMoney ledger entry ('shop'), applyNeedsDelta hunger/mood/energy, clock +1 slot, notify + autosave; delivery-aware ledger label when buying remotely. Wired into BOTH the 3D activities sheet (Mama's menu at the Food Strip, market fresh at Kpata) and the 2D map location sheet — one store action, two surfaces. Job boards added at food-area + transport-area (4 physical boards total).

Stage Summary:
- INC 3 COMPLETE. Lokoja now has REAL geography: the Confluence is north-east where the Niger (from the NW) meets the Benue (from the E), the combined river flows south along the town's east side, Mount Patti guards the west with a switchback trail to Lugard's Rest House — and the 2D map and 3D world are drawn from the same data so they can never disagree. The city is alive (grass, palms, boats, stadium, mosque, church, traffic on real bridges) and the game loop grew a real Stage-4 need: eating (cash → hunger → clock → ledger), reachable from the 3D world AND the map. Task ID: 9
Agent: Main Agent (Super Z)
Task: "server is dead" — diagnose why the dev server / preview (port 3000) is down and restore it

Work Log:
- Verified no process was listening on 3000; preview proxy (Caddy on :81) returning 502 (backend down).
- Reconstructed platform architecture from /start.sh: at container boot, start.sh -> .zscripts/dev.sh (bun install, db:push, bun run dev, health check) launches the dev server parented to the init/caddy tree — that ancestry is immune to session cleanup. Boot timeline (/tmp/boot-timeline.log) confirms this succeeded at 08:20 today (19s, healthy).
- The boot server was stopped externally (~12:54, platform dev.pid written) — consistent with the session handoff when the previous conversation ran out of context.
- Empirical killer isolation: any process holding a TCP LISTEN socket spawned from an agent Bash session (setsid'd next dev on 3000; python http.server on 3000, 3999, and 127.0.0.1:3444) is killed between tool invocations; a non-listening setsid sleep survives. Conclusion: agent-spawned servers CANNOT persist — only boot-ancestry servers can. Not OOM (cgroup failcnt 0, 4GB limit), not a universal reaper.
- Ruled out self-service revivals: no sudo for z, no cron/atd/systemd/tmux, MCP control service (12600) exposes only the standard tool set (no exec/restart tool), Caddy admin API not exposed.
- Verified restart safety: git working tree clean at f93d144 (Stage 8 complete), db/custom.db present, `bun run db:push` exits 0, bun 1.3.14 + .zscripts/dev.sh intact — the boot flow is guaranteed to relaunch the server on container restart.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause: platform stopped the boot-time dev server during the previous session's handoff; agent-spawned listeners are policy-killed between tool calls, so the preview cannot be revived from inside the session.
- Fix: user restarts the sandbox (restart button) -> start.sh reruns .zscripts/dev.sh -> dev server returns on 3000 sanctioned and stable. All code/data verified safe for restart (clean tree, committed Stage 8).

Task ID: 10
Agent: Main Agent (Super Z)
Task: MILESTONE 1 (partial — roads first) — "begin with the roads, perfect them with street lights, no houses on the road, add the bridges, well routed and built"

Work Log:
- DIAGNOSTIC FIRST (scripts/diag-road-clearance.ts): measured the state the user complained about — 10 of 18 buildings had corners ON the rendered carriageway (worst −3.0u inside), and 235 of 244 street lamps stood INSIDE the carriageway (lamp offset 2.6u < highway render half-width 3.1u). The user's eye was right.
- ROAD-CLEARANCE LAW (layout.ts): new roadGap/roadGapMajors (distance to nearest RENDERED carriageway edge, render-width-aware) + buildingClearOfRoad (worst-case corner radius must clear every edge). isClearOf rewritten on top; tryPlace rewritten as a deterministic exhaustive ring scan (24 spokes × radius steps, first legal fit — placement density survived the stricter law).
- pushSafe for every anchor-placed signature building (family house, market sheds, civic block, colonial relics, hostel): if the hand-set spot violates the law it slides outward on a deterministic spoke scan (14 steps × 12 dirs); if no legal spot exists the building is SKIPPED — nothing stands on the road, period.
- STREET LIGHTS OF LOKOJA rebuilt in layout.ts (replaced thin GEO_LAMPS): every lit road class end-to-end (highway 13u spacing, major 14, secondary 15, minor 19 through the built-up fabric within 58u of districts), alternating sides, offset = renderHalf + 0.62, arm yaw facing the carriageway, tall masts on highways. Rim-fallback ladder (near rim → far rim → opposite side) so dual-carriageway highways keep their lighting on the outer rim. Dry-ground check (heightAt > WATER_Y + 0.15) + global roadGap ≥ 0.3 + school-yard clip + dedupe. Bridge abutment masts: every bridge corner searched on an along×across grid for a dry, clear spot (masts placed before road lamps, dedupe-bypassed). Result: 440 lamps (was 244), 0 inside any carriageway (was 235).
- CityMesh Lamps() renderer upgraded: pole + arm reaching OVER the road + cowl + sodium head + warm ground pool at the arm tip (was: bare pole + head). Tall highway masts 5.1u/1.5u arm. Both head materials glow at night (found + fixed: mast heads previously had no emissive ref — never glowed).
- LANE MARKINGS (WorldTerrain Roads()): worn-white dashed centrelines on every asphalt highway/major/secondary (1.5u dash / 2.2u gap) + solid dual edge lines on highways; clipped at school fences, skipped over bridge spans and water. One merged draw call.
- E2E hook: __lkl.rest / bridges / timeOfDay added (honest clock slots + real bridge data for test drives).
- Tests: world3d suite extended with the M1 law blocks — road clearance (0 building corners on tarmac, stalls, lamps), street lights (≥350, facing, tall-mast split, dry ground, school-yard clip), bridges (16 real crossings, decks land on solid banks, decks above waterline, urban bridges lit at both abutments). - systems/fortress.ts (pure): a pure-TS SHA-256 (FIPS 180-4 verified against node:crypto: 'abc' → ba7816bf…, '' → e3b0c442…, utf-8 ₦ survives; block-boundary sweep 55→200 bytes all match) + HMAC-SHA256 (RFC 2104, RFC 4231 case-1 vector b0344c61… — the inner digest feeds back as 32 RAW bytes, the bug that taught us); signPayload/verifyPayload (constant-time-ish walk, tampered | missing-key | malformed verdicts — THE MERCY LAW: a missing device key never damns a save, it re-keys as legacy); THE KOBO-CHAIN (chainHash = H(prev|day|slot|amount|category|label), seed-anchored, order-sensitive, relabel-sensitive, legacy-tolerant before the first signed link, mint-injection caught at the exact entry); THE INCOME GUARD (single ceiling ₦1.5M, day ceiling ₦3M — both far above every legit flow incl. the ₦150k send-off; overflow clamps + flags, negative refused at the door); the velocity guard (24 tx/slot flood gate, symmetric on spend); the anomaly verdict (honest-but-weird continues; rollback is the transport's job); the config fingerprint (sha256 over the critical economy tables, 16-hex, order-honest); THE LOAD PLAN (primary → shadow → backup → fresh, narrating every descent) + the write plan (the shadow receives the CURRENT primary before the overwrite)
- save.ts v15: the SIGNED ENVELOPE ({fmt:2, data, sig:HMAC}) over the exact serialized save, keyed by a device-local key stored OUTSIDE the save (FORT_KEY_STORAGE, crypto.getRandomValues, test hook setFortKey); atomic double-buffer writes; the load descent re-verifies EVERY step and — the bug the battery caught — never demotes tampered bytes into the shadow (the broken primary is dropped BEFORE the recovery write); lastTransportVerdict narrates (ok | legacy | missing-key | recovered-shadow | recovered-backup | fresh); SAVE_VERSION 15 (+v14 migration, the fortress slice healed)
- Types v15: FortressState (anomalies, dayEarned ledger, slot flood counter, chainTail, fingerprint) + Transaction.sig (the chain link, undefined = legacy)
- Store: addMoney/removeMoney/giveReward ALL route through the velocity gate + the income guard + the kobo-chain (the clamp pays honestly to the line and COUNTS; the juice pops the PAID amount); saveNow embeds the config fingerprint; pushCloud carries the envelope's sig; the transport changed for every surface (patchSettings/backup load through the same law)
- API: CloudSave.sig column (+db push + generate); push stores it, pull + login return it — tamper-evidence rides back beside the save
- UI: the Profile ("You") tab gains the 🛡️ Fortress row (SECURE/CHAIN chip walks the live ledger chain via verifyChain; anomalies counted honestly)
- BUGS CAUGHT: the HMAC inner-digest-as-hex-string bug (the RFC vector caught it — raw 32 bytes is the law); the recovery write demoting tampered bytes into the shadow (the CHEAT-1 aftermath check caught it); the test's own wrong RFC data ("Hi" vs "Hi There"); direct bun PrismaClient writes alongside the live server leave SQLite read-only for the server's connection (second dev-server restart — M14 discipline: test through HTTP only); the m9/m7/m8/m10/m11/m12 raw localStorage parses needed the envelope unwrap; the E2E forgot 'Continue your life' after the boot reload (nav tabs came back empty — the menu was showing)

Stage Summary:
- M14 COMPLETE. The city's books are sealed: every save is HMAC-signed against a device key that lives outside the save, every ledger entry chains from the seed (an edited kobo breaks the walk at the exact entry), every payout rides the income guard (ceilings far above honest life, clamps counted as anomalies), every write double-buffers (a torn tab can no longer eat a save), and the vault carries tamper-evidence beside every cloud row. The cheat battery ends every classic hack with the same verdict: the honest cash survives.

Agent: general-purpose (M19 audio swarm)
Task: music.ts procedural afro-fusion + haptics.ts + ambience extension

Work Log:
- Absorbed house style from audio.ts/ambience.ts + the makeRng mulberry32 pattern from world3d/layout.ts (copied, not imported — systems stays decoupled from the world module)
- systems/music.ts (NEW, ~430 lines): procedural afro-fusion soundtrack, WebAudio, ZERO assets. C-major-pentatonic MUSIC table (scaleHz/bpm per mood 108-100-92-78/maxVoices 8/lookahead 0.3s/tick 100ms); patternFor(mood, bar) is a PURE deterministic method — FNV-1a hash of "mood:bar" seeds a mulberry32, 16 sixteenth-steps of kick (afro bedrock 0/7/10 + seeded ghosts), off-beat hats with seeded fills, syncopated bass roots on an I–I–V–IV-style walk through the pentatonic (C C A G), sparse kalimba lead drawn ONLY from scaleHz × {0.5,1,2} (morning biased an octave up, night 68% rests). Engine: lazy ctx (user-gesture law), master 0→0.14 fade over 1.2s, ONE setInterval scheduler on the 16th grid, idempotent start per mood, mood change = crossfade on the SAME ctx (grid reset + duck/rise), document.hidden → scheduler goes silent but pushes the grid forward (no catch-up burst), stop() clears timer/disconnects/closes. THE VOICE CAP: live voices counted by end-time stamps pruned per tick; scheduling order IS the drop order (kick → bass → lead → shaker → hat) so at cap the quietest layers die first (hat → shaker → lead). setIntensity is the extras-first governor hook, read per bar: level 0 = bass+lead only, 1 = no shaker, 2 = full kit. Shared nodes: one noise buffer, one bass lowpass 400Hz, one hat highpass 6kHz, one shaker bandpass 2kHz — no per-note filters; mood gain table (night 0.7 = lofi hush)
- systems/haptics.ts (NEW, ~60 lines): THE HAPTIC LAW — fire(event) vibrates at most once per event, guarded typeof navigator/vibrate, silently no-op on desktop; HAPTIC_PATTERNS exact (reward 18, levelup [30,40,60], arrival [15,30,15], festival [20,30,20,30,40], quest [25,25,25], secret [10,20,10,20,50]); setEnabled + setElder (elder force-mutes inside fire() regardless of enabled — belt + braces with the store gate); zero timers, two booleans of state, failures swallowed
- systems/ambience.ts (SMALL EDIT): two new layers in the exact layer() pattern — horns (bandpass 520 Q2.5, gain = traffic × 0.05 × outdoor) + zinc (bandpass 3200 Q1.2, 0.05 while rainIndoors); AmbienceParams extended with OPTIONAL rainIndoors?/traffic?, defaulted inside update() via the p2 spread so existing callers compile untouched; new scheduleHorns(buf) on one 4-9s random timer: horn stab = detuned ~2% saw pair 300-500Hz, 0.18s decay, gain 0.035 × traffic, only when traffic > 0.25 && !night; zinc tick accents ride the same tick (30% chance, 4ms noise slice bandpass 4-6kHz, gain 0.02); horn timer cleanup added to stop(); market layer left strictly as-is
- Verified with a throwaway bun check (no files touched): patternFor deterministic across 4 moods × 12 bars (deep-equal twice), every bass/lead hz ∈ scaleHz × {0.5,1,2}, night lead sparser than morning (47 vs 91 notes/9 bars), morning octave-up share 0.77, HAPTIC_PATTERNS exact, haptics.fire silent no-op without navigator
- tsc: ZERO errors in music.ts/haptics.ts/ambience.ts. The working tree shows 16 error sites — the 9 pre-existing PLUS 7 from the swarm's own in-flight types.ts/save.ts/store/test edits (GameSettings music/haptics literals, GameSave story/patti/wardrobe) in files owned by other tasks; none caused by this task (both new files are imported by nobody yet; the ambience change is additive-optional)

Task ID: 3-b
Agent: roads-data subagent
Task: kerb + zebra crossing data module (world3d/roads.ts)

Work Log:
- Studied layout.ts (ROADS/RoadDef ~389, ROAD_RENDER_W ~408, seg-grid + roadGap/roadDistance ~418-535, riverInfo ~342, heightAt ~834, inSchoolYard/inCivicCampus ~556/585, BRIDGES ~980, WATER_Y/WALK_BOUND/DISTRICTS) and WorldTerrain.tsx junctionDiscs (~541, endpoint-cluster algorithm) + markings builder (~584, dash/edge segment walk, yaw = atan2(bx-ax, bz-az)).
- NEW src/game/world3d/roads.ts — pure deterministic data, imports ONLY from './layout', no three.js/React/DOM; reuses layout's own seg-grid query fns (roadGap/riverInfo/heightAt), builds no new grid.
- KERB_RUNS: for each asphalt artery (highway/major/secondary — 102 roads: 60/31/11), resample the polyline every 4u (arc-position walk, per-segment unit perpendicular like the markings builder), candidate points at ±(ROAD_RENDER_W[cls]/2 + 0.34); a point is valid only if heightAt > WATER_Y+0.12, !inSchoolYard && !inCivicCampus, riverInfo.d > halfWidth+1.0 (bridge spans excluded — bridges carry their own structure), roadGap >= 0.05 (junction mouths break the kerb open), hypot <= WALK_BOUND. Contiguous same-side chains (≤5u) emit one KerbRun per consecutive pair. CHORD LAW added after my own sweep caught 22 corner-cutting segments: a prospective segment's midpoint must pass the same laws or the run terminates (sharp-corner chords otherwise slice across the carriageway they line). Cap 6000 not reached (204).
- ZEBRAS: cluster the 204 first/last endpoints of all r.major arteries within 5u (exact junctionDiscs algorithm, discovery order, ≥2 members → 39 clusters / 197 clustered endpoints); one crossing per entering arm at clusterCentre − dir*(renderHalf+2.2) with dir = unit(centre→endpoint) (degenerate |CE|<0.25 falls back to the arm's own first/last segment direction), rotY = atan2(dirX,dirZ), half = renderHalf. Laws: riverInfo.d > halfWidth+1.5, !inSchoolYard && !inCivicCampus, heightAt > WATER_Y+0.12, roadGap(centre) <= 0.6 (must sit ON the carriageway mouth); dedupe < 4u against accepted zebras; cap 48. Pipeline truth: 39 clusters → 137 law-valid → 72 dedupe-rejected → 65 accepted → 48 shipped (cap fired).
- DIAGNOSTIC (probe, /tmp only): classified the 909 roadGap rejections — 97% are OTHER roads' carriageways (real junction mouths in the dense fabric), 26 own-road corner fillets; water 17 / yard 164 / river 2 / bound 43. Kerb fragmentation (avg run ≈ 2 points) is the honest output of the mouth-open law on Lokoja's real street density.
- HEADLESS GATE (repo law, documented in-file): layout.ts module scope requires filled geo arrays, so bare `require(roads.ts)` throws at layout's CONFLUENCE_POINT — the task's literal verify command cannot work for ANY layout-derived module. Used the established pattern: require('scripts/geo/bootstrap.ts') FIRST (reads public/world/geo-v1.bin from disk, initGeoWith) then require(roads.ts) — same guarantee the browser geo gate gives the world chunk. In-app, roads.ts must be imported from behind the geo gate (the world chunk) like layout.ts/schools.ts.
- VERIFY: kerb runs 204, points 434, roads {"highway":60,"major":31,"secondary":11}, truncated false; zebras 48 {"highway":24,"major":20,"secondary":4}; reimport (cached) 0ms. Full law sweep: 0 violations across both endpoints AND midpoints of all 204 emitted runs (valid-point roadGap min 0.050 = exactly the floor, max 0.584) and all 48 zebra centres (roadGap −3.034..0.462, all ≤ 0.6); min pairwise zebra distance 4.05 (dedupe holds); cap-fresh module deterministic. bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors in src/ (remaining tsc noise is pre-existing vendored research/competitor-zip, not src/). eslint clean on the new file.

Stage Summary:
- world3d/roads.ts ships KERB_RUNS (204 segments / 434 chained points along both edges of all 102 asphalt arteries; runs 6000-cap not reached) and ZEBRAS (48 crossings at major-artery junction mouths; 48-cap fired from 65 accepted). Laws enforced — kerb point+chord: heightAt > WATER_Y+0.12, outside school yards + civic campus, riverInfo.d > river halfWidth+1.0 (no bridge spans), roadGap ≥ 0.05 (junction mouths stay open), within WALK_BOUND, 5u chain limit; zebra: river clearance +1.5, yard/campus exclusion, water margin, roadGap ≤ 0.6 (on-tarmac mouth), 4u dedupe, order-deterministic caps. Everything grid-reuse (no new spatial index), O(n) over 1,624 candidates + 204 endpoints, import-time cost ≈ tens of ms, cached reimport 0ms. Known honest limits: zebras hit the 48 cap (deterministic ROADS-order truncation — byClass counts reflect the cap, not the fabric); kerb runs are short because every minor-street mouth legally breaks them; the renderer (future task) must drape/extrude and is free to round chain corners.

Work Log:
- NEW src/game/world3d/junctions.ts (pure data + laws): 39 junction mouth clusters from the real major-artery endpoint graph (junctionDiscs law, 34 with ≥2 major arms). TRAFFIC LIGHTS: 12 masts at the biggest mouths (siting search: 4 arm distances × 2 sides × 3 verge offsets, roadGap ≥ 0.45, off yards/water). THE PHASE LAW: green 12s → amber 3s → red 15s (30s cycle), phase = pure function of running world seconds + position-hashed offset — 10 distinct offsets, the grid never runs one green wave. SPEED BUMPS: 6 humps (2 per school × 3 frontages) at the artery sample nearest each school gate — h(u) = A·cos²(π·u/2), A = 0.09, exactly 0 at the ends (the exact-endpoint clamp — my own suite caught cos(±π/2) float dust), C¹ smooth, monotone halves; bumpHeightAt(x,z) is THE shared truth (renderer extrudes it, physics rides it, suite verifies it). DANFO STOPS: 3 placed at transit bays (danfo/taxi/keke/okada of BAY_SPECS; private sedans get none) — the siting search steps behind/beside each bay until roadGap ≥ 0.6 (measured: bay shoulders sit ON junction tarmac bowls — 2 bays honestly have no legal furniture ground and get no stop).
- THE ACKERMANN LAW: rigs rebuilt — danfo/sedan/lorry front wheels now ride in yaw PIVOTS (6 steerable wheels); DriveSystem computes the turn radius R = |speed/yawRate| per frame and lays the REAL angles δi = atan(L/(R−w/2)), δo = atan(L/(R+w/2)) on [left, right] frontWheels (per-model L/w measured off the rigs: taxi 2.56/1.6, danfo 2.7/1.72), fork vehicles (okada/keke) steer by the bicycle-model δ = atan(L/R). The suite verifies cot(δo) − cot(δi) = w/L to 1.4e-14 across the fleet grid and that the inner wheel always steers sharper. The old single-angle `steer.rotation.y = turnRate·0.35` hint is gone — real geometry replaced it. AI traffic (Actors.tsx) only touches rig.steer on fork vehicles — untouched, verified.
- BUMP PHYSICS: the drive rig rides bumpHeightAt (body lift = the shared profile) and sheds speed on the throat (×1.6·dt·h/A — a real hump slows the car; painted-only lies don't).
- RENDERERS: CityMesh TrafficLights — instanced mast+head (merged unit), three unlit lamp discs per light (vertical red/amber/green head), instanceColors rewritten ONLY on phase change (between changes: 12 comparisons, zero work); lamp matrices set once on the first live frame (the react-hooks/immutability rule correctly rejected the effect+frame shared-ref mutation — moved into the loop behind a didSetup gate). - THREE LAWS THE BUILD TAUGHT (all caught by evidence, all fixed at the root):
  1. ENGINE.IO PATH LAW — with socket.io path '/', engine.io claims EVERY url (startsWith('/')) and a plain-HTTP health door cannot exist beside the wire; the earlier curl "success" was engine.io's 400 body. Fixed: default '/socket.io' path (gateway routes on the QUERY, not the path — proven by curl before trusting it).
  2. PRISMA DATETIME STORAGE LAW — Prisma's SQLite connector stores DateTime as INTEGER epoch-ms; the wire's first writes used TEXT and POISONED 18 LiveEvent rows (Prisma read: "Inconsistent column data: Conversion failed"). Fixed at the root (wire writes Date.now()) + a one-time idempotent repair (scripts/repair-liveevent-createdAt.ts, 18 rows converted, Prisma read re-proven green). The suite's wizard-account insert had the same bug — fixed too.
  3. THE ZOMBIE SHUTDOWN LAW — the SIGTERM handler used httpServer.close(cb) which waits for open websockets: the callback never fires, the process becomes a zombie (listener closed, sockets alive), and the client NEVER learns the wire died (the witness caught the chip refusing to flip; the probe caught marks:[] with the server demonstrably dead). Fixed: die loud on signal — the client gets the RST, flips to degraded, the relay takes over. Also: one malformed packet (a body called as an ack fn) killed the whole service early on — THE NATION STANDS LAW now pins that no single packet can.
- W4 CLIENT — src/game/systems/wire.ts: ONE singleton socket (idempotent wireEnsure, re-handshake on token rotation), honest states off/connecting/live/degraded, seq cursor + feed dedupe (cap 200), resume-on-reconnect inside the 3s gate (reconnectionDelayMax 3000), idle traffic = one ping/12s (N5), wireForget off-switch, wireSendChat/wireSendEmote/wireSendDm/wireOpenDm with the SAME verdict shapes the relay lane returns.

## 1. THE PRIOR RUN'S LANDED WORK — INVENTORY (re-verified from disk, all live)

KEY LAW rlKey = `${kind}:${part}|${part}` (identity handle AND IP throttled together; absent part '-'); ipOf = first x-forwarded-for hop, else x-real-ip, else 'local'. FAIL-SAFE: MAX_KEYS 10,000 + opportunistic prune. Constants RL_LIMITS: LOGIN 4 fails/15min, 30s→15min; TIER 10/min; PIN 4 fails/15min, 30s→15min; PUSH 30/h; PULL 60/h; REGISTER 10/15min.

## 2. THE CLIENT RECONCILIATION (this run — the never-landed half)
Post-split locations (grep-verified: trade/dues client logic lives ONLY in slices/net.ts; economy.ts holds the pocket engine addMoney/removeMoney and no trade code):
- net.ts:97-107 (liveSync trade branch) · :244-253 (livePayDues) · :287-293 (liveOfferTrade) · :296-319 (liveAcceptTrade).
DIFFS (every hunk marked `// PH2.5-ESCROW-CLIENT:` or `// PH2.5-DUES-CLIENT:` + reason):
1. net.ts:55 — import { useWalletNetStore, walletNetSync } from '../../systems/walletnet' (the purse mirror + its refresh/pull API; the file's own SERVER-WINS law).
2. net.ts:64-75 — module helper mirrorServerBalance(b): finite-number check then `useWalletNetStore.setState({ balance, syncedAt, lane: 'relay' })` — the exact applyAck write walletnet already runs; returns false (caller falls back to a full walletNetSync() pull) when an ack carries no usable balance.
3. livePayDues — the client `removeMoney(amount, 'Union dues …')` DELETED (it was a SECOND, pocket-side debit against the server purse debit at route :293-297). Now: ack balance → mirror (fallback walletNetSync). Notify fires unconditionally (res.ok proves the purse debit committed). Header law: "the relay books the move — the SERVER purse pays it".
4. liveOfferTrade — no client debit existed (correct), ADDED the missing mirror refresh from the ack's post-escrow `balance` (fallback walletNetSync) — the offerer's purse display now reflects the escrow the moment it lands.
5. liveAcceptTrade — the client `addMoney(t.giveCash)` DELETED (double-paid the server credit inside the accept tx). KEPT `removeMoney(t.wantCash)` (the one client-held leg; wantCash escrow = documented remaining debt). ADDED replay gate: `res.replayed === true` moves NOTHING (the old code re-charged wantCash AND re-counted tradesDone on a replayed ack; appliedTrades book now also guards the ack path itself). Mirror takes the ack's post-credit `balance`. Notify copy now says the giveCash entered the NATION PURSE.
6. liveSync trade branch — the OFFERER's move `(wantCash - giveCash)` → `+wantCash` only (the -giveCash half is server-true: escrowed at offer); the RECIPIENT's move `(giveCash - wantCash)` → `-wantCash` only (the +giveCash half is server-true: credited at accept); both only when wantCash > 0. The negative-moves loop labels itself with m.why now (a synced trade payment was booked under the hardcoded 'Market Union fine — the round still paid' label AND bumped finesPaid + fired the ⚖️ court toast — the finesPaid/fine-notify legs ride a new `fine: true` flag on the court move only). After any synced trade: `void walletNetSync()` — the mirror re-pulls server truth (purse naira moved server-side).
7. Header comment updated: the file is no longer claims "zero logic changes" — this reconciliation IS the one deliberate post-split change.
Return shapes unchanged (types.ts NetSlice untouched). Import cycle useGameStore → net.ts → walletnet → useGameStore is safe (lazy derefs only; proven by boot + headless store probe).

## 3. SMALL-GAP FIXES (surface sweep follow-ups, route "fixes only")
- /api/cloud/push/route.ts — GAP FOUND by the mandate's own probe: `{player:{cash:1e308}}` pushed OK (finite, ≥0 — the F1 law never had an upper bound) and the delta path had NO scalar law at all (a patch could rebuild a hostile envelope straight into the vault). FIX: MAX_SAVE_CASH ₦1,000,000,000 + MAX_SAVE_DAY 36,500 (honest play is orders of magnitude below: ledger caps ₦50k single/₦200k day) enforced by a shared saveScalarsOk() on BOTH write paths; delta violation → 400 'patch refused — bad payload'. Note: client-side heal (save.ts:631 `Math.max(0, Math.floor(cash))`) still lets 1e308 through ON LOAD — src/game/systems/** is outside this run's editable set; flagged for the lead (server now refuses to STORE it; the pull door streams only vault-legal rows).
- /api/auth/tier/route.ts — GAP: the throttle keyed on IP ONLY (`rlKey('tier',[ip])`) although the body DECLARES a handle — rate-limit.ts's own KEY LAW says both halves throttle TOGETHER; IP-only let one handle be brute-forced from N IPs at 10/min each. FIX: `rlKey('tier',[handle||null, ipOf(req)])` (login door's shape) + body read ONCE (the old gate would have double-consumed the Request stream; malformed JSON is now an honest 400, was a 500). Probed: 401×10 → 429 at 11-12; garbage body → 400.
- Known debts documented, deliberately NOT fixed (out of "small" scope): tier stays a fixed-window probe door (no failure lockout — 10/min/IP+handle aggregate; a 4-digit PIN space needs ~17h/IP); forge/vote POST cap is racy without a UNIQUE(accountId,city) index (needs a migration; Phase-2 known debt); live/action + live/sync carry no per-account VOLUME cap on non-PIN actions (the PIN lockout + ledger caps + take-limits bound the money surfaces; chat bounded by sanitize/canLive); assoc-withdraw credits the member's POCKET client-side while the treasury decrements server-side (cross-ledger, pre-existing, symmetric purse-credit would be the fix); telemetry/profile/wire-status are uncapped but write-bounded by shape (upsert 1/account/day, verdict-capped profile, 1.5s-timeout localhost health pass-through).

| 11 | telemetry | closed counter enum, 0..9999 clamps, ≤2KB, day 1..100000 | bearer + kid wall 403 | NONE — documented (upsert 1/account/day bounds flood to re-writes) | 1 upsert keyed accountId_day |

Every money surface (2,3,4,5,6,8's wallet/trade/dues legs) is authed + throttled; the residuals in 7-12 are volume-shape-bound and documented above.

## 5. REGRESSION (final code state)
- bunx tsc --noEmit → exit 0, ZERO errors.
- bunx eslint src/app/api src/game/store/slices src/lib --quiet → exit 0, empty.
- bun scripts/audit-tmp/bravo/econ-exec.ts → 37 PASS / 1 FAIL — the 1 is the PRE-EXISTING documented harness artifact ('FAIL ATM ∞ on normal day → got=5000 want=null'), identical to the Phase-2 + Foxtrot baselines; conservation law intact. NOT a regression.
- bun scripts/audit-tmp/f2/busy-guard-check.ts → ALL F2 CHECKS PASS (20/20) — boots the real composed store headless WITH the reconciled net.ts + the new walletnet import cycle: store boots, busy-guards/POS-levy/pull-gate all green.
- bun scripts/audit-tmp/bravo25/client-recon-check.ts → ALL CLIENT-RECON CHECKS PASS (24/24, see verbatim above) — the reconciliation PoC: real store, relay-shaped fetch stubs.
- NO dev server, NO full build (per mandate).

## 6. DB HYGIENE
- db/custom.db backed up to scripts/audit-tmp/bravo25/custom.db.bak BEFORE any DB test (sha256 a3a2a004…, the prior 13:25 bak had drifted — refreshed), ALL 9 probes run against the REAL db through the REAL route handlers, then RESTORED: final sha256 == backup sha256 (test rows — bravo25_* accounts, wallets, trades, vault rows, tier rows — wiped).
- Evidence: scripts/audit-tmp/bravo25/{rate-limit-probe,escrow-poc,bruteforce-poc,payload-probe,sync-probe,hostile-1e308,hostile-day,tier-key-probe,client-recon-check}.ts + their .out verbatim captures + custom.db.bak.

Stage Summary (final security posture per surface):
- auth/login — brute-force proof: 401×4 → 429 at 5-6, correct creds refused during lockout, Retry-After rides. STRONG.
- auth/register — 10/15min/IP row-growth wall + full regex/profile laws + M13 minor shield. STRONG.
- auth/tier — 10/min handle+IP (fixed this run), parent-PIN gate. GOOD (no failure lockout — documented).
- cloud/push — 30/h + 512KB ×3 doors (envelope/patch/rebuild) + full scalar law incl. the NEW cash/day ceilings on both write paths. STRONG (hostile 1e308 now dies at 400 whole AND via delta).
- cloud/pull — 60/h + oversized-row refusal. STRONG.
- live/action — bearer identity law; escrow proven end-to-end on the real DB: offer debits, accept credits EXACTLY once (sequential + concurrent replay), cancel refunds exactly, overdraft ×2 one winner, TTL sweep refunds, conservation A+B+C+held == entered; dues debit the server purse behind a tail-CAS tx; PIN lanes lockout-proven; bounded takes everywhere. STRONG (no volume cap on non-PIN actions — documented).
- live/sync — bounded heartbeat: prune-proven (dead rows leave with the reading request), retention hourly, takes 120/200. GOOD (no volume cap — documented).
- forge/vote / telemetry / profile / wire-status — thin doors, bounded by shape; volume caps absent (documented, impact-bounded by upsert/verdict/timeout laws).
- CLIENT (the reconciliation) — money is neither double-paid nor destroyed: dues + trade giveCash are SERVER purse truth (pocket never self-applies them; mirror takes the ack balance, walletNetSync re-pulls after syncs); the pocket's only live legs are wantCash (paid once, replay-safe) and court fines; the offer path debits nothing client-side. Proven 24/24 headless on the real store.

## TASK 5-e — TEAM ECHO: DEVICE MATRIX VALIDATION (Phase 2.5) — PRE-REGISTERED CRITERIA
Agent: Team Echo · Date: 2026-10-21 · Status: WAITING FOR ALPHA (:3200 marker not up yet; poller running)
Editable set respected: scripts/audit-tmp/echo25/** ONLY (this harness + results). No src edits. No build unless Alpha fails.

### PRE-REGISTERED THRESHOLDS (stated BEFORE any run — no post-hoc goalpost moves)
Verdict per tier (PASS / MARGINAL / FAIL), justified ONLY by these numbers:
- FPS (30s in-world; verdict on the WALK segment = worst case; rAF-cadence FPS on mid/high where frameloop=always,
  draw-burst rendered-FPS on low where frameloop='demand' paces at 30Hz — rAF alone lies on demand tiers):
  - LOW:  walk fps p95 ≥ 25 (30fps-tier bar from the mandate) AND walk avg ≥ 18 → PASS;
          p95 ≥ 18 but < 25 → MARGINAL; below → FAIL.
  - MID:  walk fps p95 ≥ 25 AND avg ≥ 30 → PASS; p95 ≥ 20 → MARGINAL; below → FAIL.
  - HIGH: walk fps p95 ≥ 45 AND avg ≥ 50 → PASS; p95 ≥ 30 → MARGINAL; below → FAIL.
- BOOT (nav start → world-scene first frame, incl. network throttling where in force):
  - LOW ≤ 8s acceptable (mandate bar) → PASS; ≤ 20s → MARGINAL; > 20s → FAIL.
  - MID ≤ 8s → PASS; ≤ 15s → MARGINAL; > 15s → FAIL. HIGH ≤ 6s → PASS; ≤ 12s → MARGINAL; > 12s → FAIL.
- INTERACTIONS (tap→first visual response, 3 interactions: open phone, open map, start travel):
  - all 3 ≤ 250ms → PASS; any ≤ 1000ms → MARGINAL; any > 1000ms or timed out → FAIL (per tier).
- MEMORY: JS heap start→end over the measured session; 3-min soak drift (end−start) ≤ +30% of start (and < +40MB absolute)
  → no leak signal; beyond → leak signal recorded (MARGINAL at best).
- CRASHES: any pageerror = counted; any page crash / WebGL context loss without restore during soak = FAIL evidence.
  Console errors: 0 = clean; >0 = listed + judged (network noise vs game faults).
- BOTH RUNS must land in the same verdict band; if they split, the WORST band is reported (conservative).
Cross-check law: Echo FPS vs Alpha FPS on the same tier agree within ~15% → credibility proof; delta reported either way.

## TASK 5-d — TEAM DELTA — menu first-load honesty (measure → cut → prove)

### 1. IMPORT-GRAPH TRUTH (scripts/audit-tmp/delta25/graph.ts — static closure of src/app/page.tsx, no build needed)
BEFORE the cut: **85 modules / 26,450 src LOC** statically reachable from the menu entry. The fat store's real puller found: **MenuAuthSheet.tsx:22** (`import { useGameStore }`) — statically imported by **MainMenu.tsx:33** (menu path). One edge dragged in: all 11 slices + shared/types + **walletnet→wire.ts (socket.io-client!)** + npcChat + hustles + jobs. Charlie's lazy-wire had cut only the vehicles door (vehicles.ts:15 `loadWire`); **the walletnet door (net.ts:55 → walletnet.ts:32 → wire.ts) kept socket.io in first load** — verified in graph, later confirmed by chunk probes.
Top static-closure offenders (LOC): schools 1,233 (←locations.ts:273 — feeds the menu's `${LOCATIONS.length}` chip), config 1,121 (GAME_META — menu render), types 1,004, economy 946, save 898, world 808, icons 805, wire 716, shared 674, net 640, calendar 622, saveBoot 620, citypack 602, locations 576.
AFTER the cut: **41 modules / 13,874 src LOC** (−47 modules, −12,576 LOC). Markers off the closure: useGameStore, wire, walletnet, npcChat. Full tables: scripts/audit-tmp/delta25/graph-baseline.out + graph-after-cut.out.

### 2. BASELINE BUILD — **BROKEN (honest finding)**
`bun run build` on the incoming tree FAILED: `ReferenceError: Cannot access 'aZ' before initialization at Module.saveBootSlice` during **prerender of "/"** — the fat-store graph (saveBootSlice composition) is in the SSR path ONLY via MenuAuthSheet's static store import, and its circular init (the documented walletnet↔useGameStore cycle, Bravo25) dies under Turbopack's single-pass SSR evaluation. **No fresh baseline measurement was possible**; last recorded green numbers stand as "before": First Load JS 343.1 KB gz + CSS 33.0 KB gz (worklog Foxtrot), 403 KB-raw first-load chunk carrying engine.io×1, 'patti'×53, crowther-memorial×4, SAVE_VERSION.
Build-lock law: acquired via the mandated loop (held holder delta25 during baseline + rebuild + measurement; released after — never blocked Alpha; /tmp/phase25-server-up untouched/absent, port 3200 never probed-kill).

### 3. THE CUT (one lazy boundary; zero runtime-path behavior change)
- **MainMenu.tsx:42-45** — `MenuAuthSheet` static import → `next/dynamic(() => import('./MenuAuthSheet')…, { ssr:false, loading: () => null })`. Verified safe first: the sheet renders ONLY at `{authOpen && <MenuAuthSheet/>}` (MainMenu:290; authOpen flips on the 🔑 click — never during first paint or SSR), and the menu's first paint reads the menu-store only (page.tsx:35-38, MainMenu:47-52). The sheet's store hooks (registerAccount/loginAccount/notify + getState().player?.avatar prefill) are all click-time. WorldPhoneLayer's separate static import is inside the Dashboard async graph — untouched.
- Side effect of the same boundary: the fat store graph left the "/" prerender path → **the build PRERENDER TDZ is FIXED** (deterministic A/B on the same tree: build#1 baseline FAIL → edit → build#2 PASS).

### 4. WHAT STAYED, AND WHY (correctness beats bytes)
- **schools.ts via locations.ts:273** — the menu stat chip renders `${LOCATIONS.length}`; school rows are pushed at locations module scope. Menu render → LEAVE. Probe: crowther-memorial×3 still in the save/locations chunk, expected and honest.
- **save.ts (898L) + SAVE_VERSION** — menu `boot()` runs `SaveSystem.load()` synchronously (save detection + settings) → LEAVE.
- **education.ts→data/schools: NOT a real candidate** — education.ts imports config.LADDER only, never schools. No edge to cut.
- **social.ts:16→npcChat: NOT cut** — NPC_CHAT is used only inside chatNpc (social.ts:71), but chatNpc returns synchronously and both call sites (Actors.tsx:519, ChatThread.tsx:185 — outside this task's editable set) read the result sync; lazification would change the public API. Moot anyway: after the store cut, npcChat is not on the first-load closure at all.
- **next.config.ts: lucide-react counted → 1 file / 1 named symbol (XIcon in ui/dialog.tsx)** — NOT named+many → `experimental.optimizePackageImports` NOT added (per program rule). Everything else left default; next.config.ts untouched.
